081945 | # EMBYFLOW_SEEK_BURST_V17_5_5_RELEASE
081946 | # EMBYFLOW_SEEK_BURST_V17_5_5_END
081947 | 
081948 | 
081949 | 
081950 | # EMBYFLOW_SEEK_ASYNC_RESTART_V17_5_6_START
081951 | #
081952 | # RCDEV6: keep the Enigma2 main loop responsive during Emby seek restarts.
081953 | #
081954 | # Real-box RCDEV5 evidence showed that the 1200 ms burst collector works,
081955 | # but a new key could appear immediately after RESULT because the restart
081956 | # path still performed synchronous HTTP cleanup/report traffic on the UI
081957 | # thread.  Native seekTo()/seekRelative() is deliberately NOT reintroduced
081958 | # here: earlier real-box diagnostics proved that the relevant direct/static
081959 | # service is not reliably moved by native Enigma2 seek.
081960 | #
081961 | # V17.5.6 therefore keeps the proven absolute Emby StartTimeTicks restart,
081962 | # but moves only network-only work off the Enigma2 main loop:
081963 | #   * DELETE of the previous ActiveEncoding -> daemon worker
081964 | #   * Playing + Progress reports -> daemon worker, with session/generation
081965 | #     guards so a superseded seek cannot resurrect a stale session
081966 | # The actual eServiceReference/playService transition remains on the Enigma2
081967 | # main thread.  No Enigma2 UI/service object is touched by the workers.
081968 | 
081969 |

081974 |             time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", time.localtime(now)),
081975 |             int((now - int(now)) * 1000),
081976 |         )
081977 |     except Exception:
081978 |         return time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
